NOTE: AVOS IS JUST THE AME OF OUR SYSTEM BUT NAELAO IS THE PRODUCT AND COMPAY NAME SO THINGS THAT MAY BE USED BY EXTERNAL USERS USE NAELAO BUT INTERNAL WE CAN MIX SINCE WE UNDERSTAND WHAT IT IS NO NEED TO CHANGE THEM SO THINGS LIEK SDK USE @NAELAO/SDK OR SOMETHING NOT @AVOS/sdk 




# AVOS AGENT SYSTEM & PLANNER-WORKER ARCHITECTURE
## Complete Structural, Operational, and Runtime Map

---

## 1. High-Level System Topology & Invariant Boundaries

AVOS decouples intent, governance, and physical execution across four operational planes:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1. EXECUTIVE PLANE (Human Intent & Sovereign Supervision)                                              │
│    • Ingress: WhatsApp Webhook, Desktop Command Input, Phone (Calls WebRTC).                           │
│    • Actors: Verified Human Owner / Department Directors + Executive Space Assistant ("The CEO").      │
│    • Responsibility: Milestone sign-off, budget envelope allocation, emergency halt protocols.        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. PLANNING & COORDINATION PLANE (The DAG State Engine)                                                │
│    • Kernel: `NaelaoAgentDO` acting as Planner Orchestrator (Cursor Swarm Lead model).                 │
│    • State: `SpaceDO.tasks` (Relational Task DAG), `PROJECT.md`, Commit Graph.                         │
│    • Responsibility: Intent decomposition, worker selection, branch allocation, consensus gate checks.  │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. WORKER EXECUTION PLANE (Asymmetric Tool-Holding Entities)                                           │
│    • Runtime Entities: Synchronous `NaelaoAgentDO` Actors (<30s) & Asynchronous Dynamic Workflows.     │
│    • Identity: Scoped manifests, immutable `SOUL.md`, ephemeral contexts (<2,000 tokens).             │
│    • Action Space: Minimalist 3+2 Tool Surface (`Bash`, `Read`, `Edit`, `Browser`, `Delegate`).        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 4. COMPUTATIONAL LADDER HYPERVISOR (The Edge Substrate)                                                │
│    • Invisible Router: Transparent dispatch under `Bash` across T1–T6.                                  │
│    • Runtimes: `@cloudflare/computer` (DO SQLite VFS), V8 Isolates, Workflows, Kitesurf, Containers.   │
│    • Egress: Outbound Workers, WriteGuard AST inspection, Gatekeeper capability tokens.                │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 2. The Planner-Worker Asymmetric Architecture

```
[ Human Input / Goal Trigger ]
              │
              ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ DEPTH 1: PLANNER / LEAD ORCHESTRATOR (`NaelaoAgentDO` via Frontier Model: Claude 3.7 / o3)              │
│ • System Prompt Context: Clean (<1,500 tokens). System directives + Architecture overview only.        │
│ • Prohibited Actions: Physically lacks file mutation tools (`Write`, raw `Edit` are stripped).         │
│ • Primary Operation: Deconstructs goal into an acyclic directed graph of tasks in `SpaceDO.tasks`.     │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                    │
                 ┌──────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────┐
                 ▼ (Topological Workstream Dispatch)                                   ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ DEPTH 2: DOMAIN SUB-PLANNERS                     │ │ DEPTH 2: DOMAIN SUB-PLANNERS                     │
│ (`NaelaoAgentDO` Fast Actor / Dynamic Workflow)  │ │ (`NaelaoAgentDO` Fast Actor / Dynamic Workflow)  │
│ Model: Fast Edge (Llama 3.3 70B / Qwen 2.5 32B)  │ │ Model: Fast Edge (Llama 3.3 70B / Qwen 2.5 32B)  │
│ Role: Workstream Leader (e.g., Backend Lead)     │ │ Role: Workstream Leader (e.g., QA / Test Lead)   │
│ Responsibility: Scopes module-level tasks, forks │ │ Responsibility: Scopes module-level tasks, forks │
│ Artifacts branches, assigns worker instances.    │ │ Artifacts branches, assigns worker instances.    │
└────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┘ └────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────┘
                         │                                                    │
       ┌─────────────────┴─────────────────┐                ┌─────────────────┴─────────────────┐
       ▼                                   ▼                ▼                                   ▼
┌───────────────────────────────┐ ┌───────────────────────────────┐ ┌───────────────────────────────┐
│ DEPTH 3: LEAF WORKER (Inst 1) │ │ DEPTH 3: LEAF WORKER (Inst 2) │ │ DEPTH 3: LEAF WORKER (Inst 3) │
│ Engine: Ephemeral V8 / Step   │ │ Engine: Ephemeral V8 / Step   │ │ Engine: Ephemeral V8 / Step   │
│ Model: Edge Free (Qwen 7B/3B) │ │ Model: Edge Free (Qwen 7B/3B) │ │ Model: Edge Free (Qwen 7B/3B) │
│ Branch: `task/api_auth`       │ │ Branch: `task/api_routes`     │ │ Branch: `task/unit_tests`     │
│ Context: Slice (<2,000 tokens)│ │ Context: Slice (<2,000 tokens)│ │ Context: Slice (<2,000 tokens)│
│ Action: `Edit`, `Read`, `Bash`│ │ Action: `Edit`, `Read`, `Bash`│ │ Action: `Edit`, `Read`, `Bash`│
│ 🛑 CANNOT DELEGATE            │ │ 🛑 CANNOT DELEGATE            │ │ 🛑 CANNOT DELEGATE            │
└──────────────┬────────────────┘ └──────────────┬────────────────┘ └──────────────┬────────────────┘
               │                                 │                                 │
               └─────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────┘
                                                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ THE ORACLE / REFEREE GATE (`SQLLogicTest`, `vitest`, Domain Lint)                                      │
│ • Deterministic execution. If test `exitCode === 0`: Fast-forward merge branch to `main`.               │
│ • If `exitCode !== 0`: Pass error stack back to Leaf Worker (Max 2 retries, then flag human review).  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Delegation Depth Rules
* **Depth 1 (Planner):** Top-level intent intake and global DAG generation. Max children: 4.
* **Depth 2 (Domain Sub-Planner):** Workstream management, multi-file AST planning, Git branch reservation. Max children: 3.
* **Depth 3 (Leaf Worker):** Pure atomic execution. **Leaf workers physically lack the `Delegate` tool.** They cannot spawn subagents, halting recursive delegation loops. Maximum tree fan-out is clamped to 12 concurrent leaf operations.

---

## 3. Worker Entities: Archetypes, Manifests, and Lifecycles

Workers in AVOS are not conversational chat personas. They are **asymmetric tool-holding functional operators**.

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                   WORKER DUAL-RUNTIME ENGINE MATRIX                                    │
├────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Dimension          │ Prebuilt System Workers              │ Developer Custom / Long-Horizon Workers    │
├────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Implementation** │ `NaelaoAgentDO` Fast Actor Instances │ Cloudflare Dynamic Workflows (Tier 3)      │
├────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Execution Base** │ In-Memory V8 Isolate (Durable Object)│ Durable Cloudflare Workflow Engine         │
├────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Latency Profile**│ Sub-50ms RPC turnaround              │ Asynchronous (Minutes to days)             │
├────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Primary Domain** │ Inter-thread queries, balance checks,│ Multi-file refactors, batch ETL pipelines, │
│                    │ project creation, Git branch setups  │ web sweeps, competitive worker races       │
├────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Context State**  │ Dynamic ephemeral context            │ `SubagentContext` (JSON-serializable only) │
└────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Declarative Worker Manifest Schema

```typescript
export interface FunctionalWorkerManifest {
  id: string; // e.g., "dev_ast_operator"
  name: string;
  role: "ARCHITECT" | "DEV_OPERATOR" | "REVIEWER" | "RESEARCHER" | "INGESTION" | "SECURITY";
  runtimeType: "FAST_ISOLATE_ACTOR" | "DURABLE_WORKFLOW";

  // Prompt Configuration
  prompts: {
    systemDirective: string;
    soulMarkdown: string; // Stored in /.avos/agents/:id/SOUL.md
    taskSpecificInstruction?: string;
  };

  // Hardware Clearance
  executionCeiling: {
    maxLadderTier: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6;
    allowedEngines: Array<"just-bash" | "pyodide" | "kitesurf" | "chromium" | "container">;
    memoryLimitMb: number;
    timeoutSeconds: number;
  };

  // Scoped Tool Envelope
  toolset: {
    hands: Array<"Bash" | "Read" | "Edit">;
    senses: Array<"Browser" | "Delegate">;
    boundVirtualClis: string[]; // e.g., ["git", "jq", "stripe"]
    boundMcpEndpoints: string[]; // e.g., ["code_graph", "ledger"]
  };

  // Security Policy
  security: {
    gatekeeperGrants: Array<{ resource: string; operations: Array<"read" | "write" | "admin"> }>;
    credentialAccessScope: "SPACE_SHARED" | "PROJECT_BOUND" | "NONE";
  };
}
```

## 4. The 6-Tier Execution Ladder & The Invisible Hypervisor

The model never selects an execution tier explicitly. It calls standard commands via `Bash`, `Read`, and `Edit`. The **Runtime Hypervisor** in `NaelaoAgentDO` dynamically routes execution based on command profiling and Jev AI predictions:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 6: CONTAINER & FULL-SYSTEM RUNTIME                                                                │
│ ├── 6A: Client In-Browser WebContainer (StackBlitz Wasm in client tab, $0 cloud compute)              │
│ ├── 6B: Cloudflare Edge Container (`ToolSandboxDO` / Docker Ubuntu via ArtifactFS FUSE)               │
│ └── 6C: Local Workstation Appliance (`LocalTunnelDO` / On-Prem GPUs & Docker)                           │
│ Native compilers (Rust, GCC, Go), Python/CUDA, Xvfb desktop GUI, npm native builds. 60s micro-sleep.   │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────▲────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                    │ (Promote if command requires native binaries/GUI)
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 5: FULL HEADLESS CHROMIUM (Browser Rendering Engine)                                              │
│ Pixel-level visual rendering, WebGL canvas validation, anti-bot challenge bypass.                      │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────▲────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                    │ (Promote if visual screenshot QA or WebGL required)
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 4: LIGHTWEIGHT AGENT BROWSER (Cloudflare Kitesurf)                                                │
│ Low-memory (30MB) edge browser. Fast DOM extraction, AXTree parsing, form submission. Sub-200ms.       │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────▲────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                    │ (Promote if external web navigation/DOM required)
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 3: DYNAMIC WORKFLOWS (Cloudflare Workflows)                                                       │
│ Multi-step durable execution, state checkpointing, cron scheduling, dormant sleeping ($0/compute).     │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────▲────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                    │ (Promote if long-running task, retry, or sleep needed)
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 2: DYNAMIC WORKER LOADER (V8 Isolate Shell / CodeMode)                                            │
│ Sub-millisecond TypeScript execution, REST fetches, in-memory Pyodide Wasm Python (<10ms, $0.00).       │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────▲────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                    │ (Promote if script logic or virtual CLI required)
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TIER 1: IN-MEMORY VIRTUAL FILESYSTEM (@cloudflare/computer + DO SQLite)                                │
│ Sub-millisecond Read, Edit (AST Search & Replace), Grep, Glob, `just-bash` POSIX execution.            │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Hypervisor Command Dispatch Rules

```typescript
// Conceptual Hypervisor Routing Logic inside NaelaoAgentDO
export async function routeCommandExecution(command: string, ctx: RuntimeContext) {
  const binary = command.trim().split(/\s+/)[0];

  // 1. In-Memory POSIX Utilities (Tier 1)
  if (["grep", "cat", "sed", "awk", "find", "jq", "diff", "ls"].includes(binary)) {
    return await ctx.runtimes.justBash.exec(command, { vfs: ctx.fs.sqliteVfs });
  }

  // 2. Virtual CLI Shims (Tier 2)
  if (ctx.virtualClis.has(binary)) {
    return await ctx.virtualClis.get(binary).exec(command, { vault: ctx.vault });
  }

  // 3. In-Memory Python Evaluation (Tier 2)
  if (binary === "python3" && !command.includes("torch") && !command.includes("cv2")) {
    return await ctx.runtimes.pyodide.eval(command);
  }

  // 4. Web Perception (Tier 4 / 5)
  if (binary === "browse" || binary === "scrape") {
    return await ctx.runtimes.kitesurf.navigate(command, { fallback: "chromium" });
  }

  // 5. Workflow Dispatch (Tier 3)
  if (binary === "schedule" || binary === "workflow") {
    return await ctx.runtimes.workflows.dispatch(command);
  }

  // 6. Native Execution Escalation (Tier 6)
  // Jev AI validates if browser WebContainer can handle it or if cloud container is required
  const canRunInBrowser = await ctx.decision.check(command, "Can run in <500MB RAM without native C++ compilation?");
  if (ctx.clientWebContainer?.isAlive() && canRunInBrowser.passes) {
    return await ctx.clientWebContainer.exec(command);
  }

  // Fallback to Cloudflare Container over ArtifactFS FUSE
  return await ctx.runtimes.cfContainer.exec(command, {
    fuseMount: ctx.fs.authoritativeBranch,
    idleTimeoutMs: 60000 // 60-second micro-sleep
  });
}
```

## 5. The Minimalist Universal Tool Surface & The JIT Extension Layer

Agents are constrained to five primitive operations:

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              THE MINIMALIST 3+2 TOOL SURFACE                                           │
├────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Tool Signature     │ Operational Specification & Contract                                              │
├────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ `Bash(cmd)`        │ Execute terminal operations, virtual CLIs, scripts, workflows, and build tools.   │
├────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ `Read(path, o, l)` │ Windowed, line-numbered file inspection. Reads directly from DO SQLite VFS.       │
├────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ `Edit(p, old, new)`│ Surgical AST block replacement. Verifies exact SHA-256 base hashes prior to write.│
├────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ `Browser(a, url)`  │ Navigates DOM, returns text AXTree by default. Saves screenshot to disk.          │
├────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ `Delegate(r, t)`   │ Typed JSON-RPC inter-agent task dispatching into Tier 3 System Consult Lanes.     │
└────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Extending Capabilities: The JIT Virtual CLI Engine
When an agent requires an external API or custom tooling not present in the base shell:
1. **Interception:** The router detects a missing command (e.g., `linear create-issue`).
2. **Synthesis:** The agent writes a self-contained TypeScript file implementing the subcommand using `ctx.vault.fetchWithAuth`.
3. **Registration:** Script is written to `/.avos/bin/linear.ts` and made executable (`chmod +x`).
4. **Execution:** The command enters `$PATH` globally across the project, executing in Tier 2 V8 Isolates in sub-5ms.

```
[ Unregistered Command: `linear create-issue --title "Bug"` ]
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1. ROUTER INTERCEPTION (`VirtualCliRouter.ts`)                              │
│    • Flags missing command; verifies `LINEAR_API_KEY` exists in Vault.      │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. AGENT WRITES SCRIPT TO `/.avos/bin/linear.ts`                            │
│    • Standard TypeScript imports `@avos/runtime`.                           │
│    • Maps flags to `https://api.linear.app/graphql` with injected token.    │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. BINARY COMPILATION IN DO SQLITE (`virtual_cli_commands`)                 │
│    • Command enters virtual `$PATH`. Future calls run in 4ms in V8.         │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 6. Senses vs. Hands: Perception Decoupling

AVOS separates action dispatching from sensory feedback to maintain context cleanliness.

### The Problem
Dumping large multimodal payloads (1280x800 screenshots = 1,600+ vision tokens) or extensive command outputs (e.g., 20,000-token `npm install` logs) directly into conversation contexts exhausts context windows and degrades reasoning quality.

### The Decoupled Perception Solution
1. **The `see` CLI Command:**  
   `browse <url>` outputs text-based Accessibility Trees (AXTree, <300 tokens) and saves screenshots to `/.avos/artifacts/screen_latest.png`. Multimodal image buffers are injected into the agent's context **only when it explicitly executes `see latest` or `see <path>`**.
2. **Strands 1,500-Token Tool Offload:**  
   Any tool execution producing $>1,500$ tokens of stdout/stderr is truncated. Full logs are offloaded to `/.avos/tmp/tool_out_*.log`. The agent receives a truncated excerpt along with the file path for windowed inspection.

```
[ Agent executes `browse https://stripe.com/pricing` ]
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TEXT AXTree RETURNED TO CONTEXT (<300 tokens)                               │
│ "Page: Stripe Pricing | [Button #1: Sign In] | [Link #2: Documentation]"    │
│ "Screenshot saved to: /.avos/artifacts/screen_latest.png"                   │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
       ┌──────────────────┴──────────────────┐
       ▼ (If text is sufficient)             ▼ (If layout/color verification is needed)
┌──────────────────────────────┐     ┌────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Agent continues in text.     │     │ Agent executes `see latest`            │
│ (Zero vision tokens burned)  │     │ ➡️ Runtime injects PNG buffer for that │
│                              │     │    single turn only.                   │
└──────────────────────────────┘     └────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 7. Multi-Worker Protocols & The Speculative Race (SR-EEO)

When tasks require parallel processing or competitive validation, the Orchestrator applies the **Speculative Race with Early-Exit Oracle (SR-EEO)** protocol (informed by Tencent's *Agent Forest* and the *SEMAP* protocol):

```
                                  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                                  │   TASK DAG ITEM: "Implement JWT Rotation"    │
                                  │   Test Oracle: `npm test tests/jwt.test.ts`  │
                                  └──────────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
                                                         │
                         ┌───────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┐
                         ▼ (Forks 3 Isolated Branches on Artifacts Git in 10ms)          ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
│ ⚡ LEAF WORKER A (Instance 1)     │   │ ⚡ LEAF WORKER B (Instance 2)     │   │ ⚡ LEAF WORKER C (Instance 3)     │
│ • Branch: `race/jwt_wa`          │   │ • Branch: `race/jwt_wb`          │   │ • Branch: `race/jwt_wc`          │
│ • Model: Qwen 2.5 Coder 32B      │   │ • Model: Llama 3.3 70B           │   │ • Model: DeepSeek R1             │
│ • Strategy: Low Temp (0.2)       │   │ • Strategy: High Temp (0.7)      │   │ • Strategy: Alternative AST diff │
└────────────────┬─────────────────┘   └────────────────┬─────────────────┘   └────────────────┬─────────────────┘
                 │                                      │                                      │
                 ▼                                      ▼                                      ▼
        Run Test Oracle                        Run Test Oracle                        Run Test Oracle
         (Exit Code 1)                          (Exit Code 0)                          (In Progress)
                 │                                      │                                      │
                 │                                      ▼ 🚨 EARLY-EXIT SHORT-CIRCUIT           │
                 │                     ┌──────────────────────────────────┐                    │
                 │                     │ 1. Oracle verified 100% green.   │                    │
                 │                     │ 2. Accepts Worker B's PR.        │                    │
                 └────────────────────►│ 3. Issues SIGKILL to A & C.      │◄───────────────────┘
                                       │ 4. Fast-forwards merge to `main`.│
                                       └──────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Deterministic Tie-Breaking Heuristic
When multiple workers satisfy the test oracle concurrently, the winner is determined by:

$$\text{Score} = \frac{\text{OraclePassRate}}{\text{LinesChanged} \times \text{ExecutionDurationMs}}$$

The solution with the fewest changed lines and fastest execution duration is selected for merging.

---

## 8. State Scoping, Universal Storage, and Actor ID Derivation

AVOS eliminates ambiguous shared state by tying scoping directly to the **Durable Object Actor ID derivation formula**.

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              THE UNIFIED SCOPING & STATE RESOLUTION ENGINE                             │
├──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Declared Scope       │ Physical Actor ID Derivation Formula     │ Physical Data Boundary               │
├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **`SPACE_WIDE`**     │ `idFromName("gadget:" + appId + ":space:"│ Root Space-Wide DO Facet SQLite DB.  │
│                      │             + spaceId)`                  │ Single shared state across projects. │
├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **`PROJECT_BOUND`**  │ `idFromName("gadget:" + appId + ":project│ Project-Scoped DO Facet SQLite DB.   │
│                      │             :" + projectId)`             │ Air-gapped to that single project.   │
├──────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **`USER_PRIVATE`**   │ `idFromName("gadget:" + appId + ":space:"│ User-Private DO Facet SQLite DB.     │
│                      │             + spaceId + ":user:" + userId│ Accessible only to Alice's sessions. │
└──────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Cross-Project Mounting
A shared capability (e.g. `crm_directory`) can be mounted by both an outbound marketing project (`growth_engine`) and an inbound support desk (`customer_bot`).
* Both projects declare `mount_scope: "SPACE_WIDE"`.
* The edge router resolves the identical Actor ID: `gadget:crm_directory:space:space_102`.
* Changes written by the marketing agent are instantly queryable by the customer support agent with **zero data synchronization lag**.

---

## 9. Full End-to-End Execution Trace

The following trace shows a complex objective executing across all architectural planes:

```
====================================================================================================
TRACE: User enters Team Room (Tier 1): "/goal Refactor billing to use Stripe CAS, verify tests, update docs"
====================================================================================================

[00:00.020] INGRESS:
  • Router parses input: Detects `/goal` command.
  • Bypasses chat: Routes directly to `NaelaoAgentDO` acting as Lead Orchestrator (Depth 1).

[00:00.150] INTENT DECOMPOSITION (System 1 & System 2):
  • Jev AI verifies command safety: `is_destructive: Noul(No, 0.01)`.
  • Frontier Model (Claude 3.7) plans Task DAG in `SpaceDO.tasks`:
    - Task 1: "Implement atomic CAS in `src/billing.ts`" [Assigned: DevOperator] [Oracle: `npm test billing`]
    - Task 2: "Update API documentation in `docs/billing.md`" [Assigned: DevOperator] [Oracle: `npm run lint`]

[00:00.400] DEPTH 2 SUB-PLANNER & SPECULATIVE DISPATCH (SR-EEO):
  • Depth 2 Sub-Planner forks Artifacts branches: `race/task1_w1` and `race/task1_w2`.
  • Spawns two Depth 3 Leaf Workers in parallel (Qwen 2.5 Coder 32B vs. Llama 3.3 70B).

[00:01.850] LEAF WORKER EXECUTION (Tier 1 & Tier 2):
  • Worker 1 reads files via `Read("src/billing.ts", 1, 120)` against DO SQLite VFS.
  • Worker 1 writes patch via `Edit("src/billing.ts", oldCode, newCasCode)`.
  • Worker 1 tests code: `Bash("npm test billing")`.
    - Hypervisor detects `npm`: Promotes execution to Tier 6 Container via ArtifactFS FUSE.
    - Container executes in 1.2s; output exceeds 1,500 tokens.
    - Strands Optimizer offloads raw output to `/.avos/tmp/tool_out_41.log`.
    - Test Oracle returns `exitCode: 0`.

[00:03.200] EARLY-EXIT SHORT-CIRCUIT & ORACLE VERIFICATION:
  • Worker 1 hits 100% green test assertions.
  • Orchestrator accepts Worker 1's branch (`race/task1_w1`).
  • Issues termination signal to Worker 2.
  • Fast-forward merges `race/task1_w1` into `main` on Cloudflare Artifacts.

[00:03.450] TASK 2 DISPATCH:
  • Worker 3 updates documentation via `Edit("docs/billing.md")`.
  • Linter oracle validates markdown syntax (Exit Code 0).
  • Fast-forward merges to `main`.

[00:03.800] COMPLETION PRESENTATION:
  • Orchestrator updates Project Journal: All tasks marked `COMPLETED`.
  • Emits WebSocket event via `WsHubDO`.
  • Renders verified Pull Request card in Tier 1 Team Room:
    [ 📬 Goal Reached: Stripe CAS Refactor Verified (14/14 Tests Passed) | Commit: `c_9e12a` ]
  • Right Sidebar Canvas Container (`CodeWorkspaceCanvas`) hot-reloads the updated files in 110ms.
====================================================================================================
```

## 10. Operational Constraints & Boundaries

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ System Layer                         │ Invariant Architectural Boundary                                │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Tool Surface**                     │ Strictly constrained to `Bash`, `Read`, `Edit`, `Browser`, and  │
│                                      │ `Delegate`. All capabilities project into terminal `$PATH`.     │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Delegation Hierarchy**             │ Depth is hard-capped at 3. Depth 3 Leaf Workers cannot delegate.│
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Git Artifacts Integrity**          │ Writes never touch `main` directly in multi-user settings;       │
│                                      │ all mutations land via Artifacts branch forks and PR cards.     │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Memory Cleanliness**               │ Chat histories never cross thread boundaries. Knowledge shifts   │
│                                      │ strictly through atomic vector rules (Cosine $>0.80$).          │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Compute Cost Floor**               │ 95% of tasks execute in V8 Isolates; containers enforce a strict│
│                                      │ 60-second micro-sleep countdown.                                │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Container State Hydration**        │ `package-lock.json` in SQLite is authoritative. `node_modules`   │
│                                      │ is ephemeral and never synchronized over the network.           │
├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ **Security Interception**            │ Destructive commands are blocked at the edge proxy by WriteGuard│
│                                      │ and System 1 Jev AI before execution can proceed.               │
└──────────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

Local Machine Access: Pluggable Skill & Multi-Tunnel CLI
The local machine must never be the hardcoded, single default environment. If it is, the agent breaks when the user is on mobile or offline, and cannot access multiple enterprise machines.
Instead, local machine access is modeled as a pluggable CLI tool and skill: tunnel.
code
Code
MULTI-TUNNEL APPLIANCE FABRIC
                                     │
                     [Agent calls `Bash` in Naelao]
                                     │
        ┌────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────┐
        ▼                            ▼                            ▼
$ tunnel exec macbook-m3    $ tunnel exec gpu-rig-01    $ tunnel exec prod-db-proxy
"npm run build"             "python3 train_lora.py"     "psql -c 'SELECT 1'"
        │                            │                            │
        ▼                            ▼                            ▼
[LocalTunnelDO: Tunnel A]   [LocalTunnelDO: Tunnel B]   [LocalTunnelDO: Tunnel C]
(User's Laptop)             (On-Premises Linux Box)     (Private Cloud Bastion)
How the Agent Uses Tunnels via Bash:
When an FDE or developer runs npx @naelao/bridge connect --name macbook-m3, an authenticated WebSocket reverse tunnel links their machine to LocalTunnelDO.
The agent discovers available tunnels using standard bash utilities:
code
Bash
$ tunnel list
AVAILABLE TUNNELS:
• macbook-m3       (Online, macOS arm64, Load: 12%)
• gpu-box-rtx4090  (Online, Ubuntu 24.04, CUDA 12.4, Free VRAM: 22GB)
The agent targets any machine explicitly:
code
Bash
$ tunnel exec gpu-box-rtx4090 "nvidia-smi --query-gpu=utilization.gpu --format=csv"
Security Isolation: If a tunnel drops or is revoked, the agent simply loses access to that remote appliance; the core Naelao space continues operating uninterrupted on Cloudflare edge.


the agent UI currently dumps { "tool": "Bash", ... } JSON objects instead of looking like a true terminal workstation.
In production AI workstations (like Cursor, Claude Code, or Windsurf), the user should never see raw JSON tool envelopes unless they explicitly expand a debug drawer. Tool calls should render as first-class terminal blocks.
code

The UI Implementation Contract:
The Streaming Terminal Widget (TerminalStreamBlock.tsx):
Uses an ANSI-to-HTML parser (or lightweight xterm.js headless canvas) inside the message stream.
As the model calls Bash(cmd), the UI displays the $ <command> prompt line in green.
stdout and stderr stream in real time as monospace text with syntax highlighting and ANSI colors (red for errors, green for test passes).
The 1,500-Token Strands Fold:
If a command outputs 
>
1
,
500
>1,500
 tokens (e.g., massive npm install logs), the terminal box automatically collapses with a visual banner:
code
Code
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ⚠️ Output exceeded 1,500 tokens (18,420 lines hidden).                          │
│ 📄 Full log written to: /.avos/tmp/tool_out_49.log [Open in Right Canvas]       │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
GenUI Cards vs. Terminal Blocks:
Terminal Blocks are for execution, scripts, tests, and bash commands.
GenUI Cards are strictly reserved for high-level interactive user decisions (e.g., [Approve Refund], [Merge Pull Request], [Pick Model]).


Planner-Worker Swarm, Entity Definition & Progressive Skills
A planner is not just a chatbot with sub-prompts. It is an Orchestration Kernel managing a swarm of distinct functional entities.
code
Code
SWARM ORCHESTRATION TOPOLOGY
                                                │
                                                ▼
                         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                         │   DEPTH 1: PLANNER ORCHESTRATOR (`SpaceDO`)  │
                         │   Model: Claude 3.7 / o3-mini                │
                         │   • Stripped of direct file edit tools       │
                         │   • Equipped with Swarm Management Skills    │
                         └──────────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
                                                │
                         ┌──────────────────────┴──────────────────────┐
                         ▼                                             ▼
          ┌─────────────────────────────┐               ┌─────────────────────────────┐
          │ TYPE A: NAELAO SUBAGENTS    │               │ TYPE B: CUSTOM WORKERS      │
          │ (`NaelaoAgentDO` Fast Actor)│               │ (Cloudflare Dynamic Workflow)│
          ├─────────────────────────────┤               ├─────────────────────────────┤
          │ • In-Memory V8 Isolate (<30s│               │ • Durable Cloudflare Engine │
          │ • Configured via UI / JSON  │               │ • Multi-step, runs for days │
          │ • Scoped manifest & tools   │               │ • Written via `@naelao/sdk` │
          │ • Atomic leaf tasks         │               │ • Untrusted custom code jail│
          └─────────────────────────────┘               └─────────────────────────────┘
The Two Distinct Worker Classes:
Type A: Naelao Subagents (NaelaoAgentDO Fast Actors)
Built for sub-30-second atomic execution.
Can be configured via the Naelao UI or generated on the fly via a JSON manifest.
Safe to run directly inside the space isolate because its tool surface is constrained.
Type B: Custom Workers (Cloudflare Dynamic Workflows)
Built for long-running, asynchronous, durable execution (multi-hour ETLs, competitive worker races, scraping sweeps).
Runs under Cloudflare Dynamic Workflows, NOT inside NaelaoAgentDO directly. This ensures that if custom developer code loops infinitely, crashes, or attempts illegal memory operations, it is sandboxed away from the core workspace state.
Progressive Skill Loading (Context Cleanliness):
Dumping the documentation of 20 different skills into an agent's prompt burns 15,000+ tokens before the agent does anything.
AVOS enforces Progressive Skill Disclosure:
code
Code
[Agent System Prompt: Skill Manifest Index Only (<250 tokens)]
├── `orchestrate_swarm`: "Spawn, link, and race multiple workers across tasks."
├── `build_workflow_worker`: "Compile a custom dynamic workflow worker using @naelao/sdk."
├── `local_tunnel`: "Dispatch bash commands to connected on-prem or remote machines."
└── `ast_grep`: "Perform syntactic search and replacement on source trees."
When the Planner needs to build a new worker or run a swarm, it calls:
code
Bash
skill use build_workflow_worker
The runtime injects the operational manual for that specific skill into context for that single turn, executes the action, and clears the context buffer on task completion.
The Workflow Builder Skill in Action:
When the Planner needs a brand-new worker that doesn't exist, it delegates to a builder script or writes the Dynamic Workflow code directly:
code
TypeScript
// Generated dynamically by Planner via @naelao/sdk:
import { WorkflowEntrypoint, WorkflowStep, WorkflowEvent } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { NaelaoContext } from "@naelao/sdk";

export class CustomEtlWorker extends WorkflowEntrypoint {
  async run(event: WorkflowEvent<{ sitemapUrl: string }>, step: WorkflowStep) {
    const ctx = new NaelaoContext(event.payload);

    // Step 1: Crawl URLs
    const pages = await step.do("fetch_sitemap", async () => {
      return await ctx.tools.browse(event.payload.sitemapUrl);
    });

    // Step 2: Sleep and respect rate limits ($0 compute)
    await step.sleep("rate_limit_cooloff", "10 seconds");

    // Step 3: Write extracted records to Space SQLite
    await step.do("persist_records", async () => {
      await ctx.storage.sqlite.execute("INSERT INTO crawled_pages VALUES (?)", [pages]);
    });
  }
}



While the previous map defined the **Agent Core, Planner-Worker Swarm, and the Compute Ladder**, AVOS has **10 other major subsystems** that each require their own dedicated **Structural, Operational, and Runtime Map**.

Here is the complete inventory of the remaining subsystem maps. You can pick them one by one to dive into their full architectural blueprints:

---

### 1. The Cloudflare Artifacts & Git Mesh Map
* **What it covers:** The Zig-to-WASM Git engine running inside Durable Objects, 10ms zero-copy micro-branching, `ArtifactFS` FUSE container mounting, upstream staleness tracking, the AI auto-rebase engine, turn-by-turn Git time machines, and reasoning-aware `git-blame`.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How commits, branches, and diffs flow between V8 memory, DO SQLite, and container disks without file corruption.

---

### 2. The 4-Tier Thread Hierarchy & Concurrency Map
* **What it covers:** The interaction mechanics between the Team Room (Tier 1), Task Workstations (Tier 2), System Consult Lanes (Tier 3), and Private Scratchpads (Tier 4).
* **Key Runtime Flow:** Optimistic Concurrency Control (OCC) with SHA-256 CAS writes in SQLite, distributed file mutexes in `ConcurrencyDO`, the Invoker-Attested Split-Actor model, and Atomic Task Leases.

---

### 3. The Mini-AGI & Hardware-Level Vector Knowledge Map
* **What it covers:** Jack Dorsey’s sovereign permission model, Cloudflare Vectorize pre-filtering with role ranks (1–5) and bitmasks, `<context_chunk>` untrusted boundary tags, and Cohere Parse 5 edge document ingestion.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** Sub-50ms KV semantic caching $\rightarrow$ Hybrid RRF (Dense Vectorize + Sparse BM25) $\rightarrow$ interactive numbered citation footnotes.

---

### 4. The Zero-Leak Vault & Edge Security Proxy Map
* **What it covers:** Universal Vault storage, Outbound Workers TLS token injection, WriteGuard SQL AST inspection, 3-tier connector scoping (`SPACE_SHARED`, `PROJECT_BOUND`, `USER_PERSONAL`), and the public-channel privacy forking bridge.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How scripts reference logical credential names while the edge network boundary injects decrypted bearer headers with zero leaks to logs or disks.

---

### 5. The Stateful Micro-Apps (Gadgets) & Durable Daemons Map
* **What it covers:** Cloudflare OS Gadgets running on Dynamic Workers with private DO Facet SQLite databases, auto-generated stateless MCP v2 `/mcp` endpoints, and bidirectional MCP-UI canvas streaming.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How background daemons (Plandex Tree-Sitter code indexers, General Ledgers, Citation Matrices) parse changes silently and expose relational data to agents over MCP.

---

### 6. The Telephony, Voice & Universal Shared State Map
* **What it covers:** Native duplex voice via `@cloudflare/voice` (Cloudflare Calls WebRTC SFU + Whisper STT + Llama 3.3 + TTS), dual-thread voice desks (management vs. call sessions), and the pluggable Space-Shared Calendar (`primary_calendar`).
* **Key Runtime Flow:** 15-second edge slot-hold mutexes in `ConcurrencyDO` preventing double-booking during high-volume voice call spikes.

---

### 7. The Inbound Webhook Ingress, Queue Buffer & Failure Defense Map
* **What it covers:** Edge Ingress Webhook Router (`workers/router/src/webhooks.ts`), edge HMAC validation, and the 13-point automation failure defense suite.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** Edge Tombstone KV filters (410/204 drops) $\rightarrow$ Cloudflare Queues rate-limiting $\rightarrow$ Time-Travel Replay Lake in R2 $\rightarrow$ Leaky-bucket pacers preventing third-party API bans.

---

### 8. The Model Studio, LoRA Distillation & Sovereign Local AI Map
* **What it covers:** Ingesting company documents, synthetic dataset generation, LoRA fine-tuning on Cloudflare Workers AI, AI Gateway `custom/*` dynamic adapter loading, and `LocalTunnelDO` on-premise GPU bridges.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How the Air-Gapped Space Compliance Lock physically shuts down cloud AI endpoints and routes prompts to local Ollama hardware.

---

### 9. The Dogfooded Enterprise Subsystems (SaaS Killer) Map
* **What it covers:** The 6 first-party business extensions built on public primitives: `ext_ledger_core` (QuickBooks killer), `ext_crm_core` (Salesforce killer), `ext_esign_core` (DocuSign killer), `ext_omnichannel_inbox` (Zendesk killer), `ext_commerce_hub` (Shopify killer), and `ext_payroll_core` (Gusto killer).
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How Jev AI RiskGuard enforces mathematical drawdown limits and how the mobile `STOP` emergency kill-switch executes across all subsystems.

---

### 10. The Marketplace Engine, Blueprints & Monetization Map
* **What it covers:** The 1-Click Venture Exporter, automated PII scrubber, 2-tier marketplace (Freemium Tiered vs. Fixed Buy-Out), 40%/20%/100% attribution splits, cumulative tier cycle quotas, and the FDE-to-Vertical-SaaS graduation pipeline.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How D1 reseller ledgers compute payouts and how the Discover Page Live Market Radar queries Cloudflare Pipelines and Apache Iceberg on R2.

---

### 11. The Self-Hosting (Homoiconic) Developer Foundry Map
* **What it covers:** The unified shell architecture (`apps/shell`), where the Developer Portal is itself an AVOS space running `tpl_dev_foundry_v1`.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How developers use their own space's `@ExtensionArchitect` to scaffold extensions, run the automated conformance suite, and publish blueprints without leaving the shell.

---

### 12. The Data Residency & Cross-Border Sovereign Compliance Map
* **What it covers:** Cloudflare Data Localization Suite (DLS), Jurisdictional Durable Objects (`jurisdiction: "eu"`), EU-locked R2 buckets, and Regional Services TLS termination.
* **Key Runtime Flow:** How European legal and healthcare spaces operate with 100% in-region data residency and zero transatlantic data egress under the EU AI Act and GDPR.

This is the **consumer "Trojan Horse"** play for AVOS. 

When you shift from thinking about corporate boardrooms to thinking about **3 billion people on WhatsApp**, the economics, the distribution, and the product positioning change completely.

Big Tech (OpenAI with "Dots", Meta with "Muse", xAI with "GrokBot") is trying to build **centralized consumer superapps**. But they all have the same fatal flaw: **they force consumers to download a new app, open a blank text box, and pay $20 to $200 a month.**

By turning AVOS into a **WhatsApp-native, template-driven OS where Forward Deployed Engineers (FDEs) build localized vertical personas**, you bypass their entire walled garden.

### 2. The Power of the Zero-UI WhatsApp Gateway

In Latin America, Europe, Africa, the Middle East, and South Asia, **the internet IS WhatsApp**. 

People don't use desktop dashboards, Slack, or complex canvas viewports for daily tasks. They message friends, pay bills, and run informal businesses directly inside WhatsApp.

#### How a Consumer Experiences AVOS in 3 Steps:
1. **The Click-to-Chat Link:** A university student, solo freelancer, or trader clicks a link on Instagram or TikTok: *“Start chatting with your 24/7 Tax & Invoice Sentry.”*
2. **Zero-Friction Instantiation:** They send a single message: *"Hi."*
   * Behind the scenes, Cloudflare edge hooks the incoming webhook.
   * A dedicated `SpaceDO` (Durable Object) spins up with a clean SQLite instance.
   * No password. No credit card form. The user's phone number is their cryptographic ID.
3. **Immediate Utility (No Blank Canvas):** The bot doesn't say *"How can I help you today?"* It executes a strict template:
   > *"Welcome to TaxSentry. Send me a photo of any receipt, an audio note of an expense, or ask me how much VAT you owe this month. I log everything automatically."*

### 4. The Economics: Why You Can Charge $5/mo and Keep 90% Margins

Why can’t OpenAI or xAI offer ChatGPT or Grok for $5 a month?

Because on every single message—even when a user just says *"Thanks!"* or *"What was that number again?"*—Big Tech runs a **massive 70B–400B parameter model across expensive GPU clusters**, burning fractions of a cent per token. At $5/month, heavy users bankrupt them.

#### How AVOS Slashes Consumer Compute Cost to <$0.50/user/month:
1. **90% of messages are handled by System 1 JEV ($0.042/M tokens):**  
   When a user sends an image of a receipt, an audio note, or a simple command, JEV classifies the intent, extracts the data, and writes it directly to the Durable Object SQLite database in **80ms without invoking an expensive generative LLM**.
2. **Edge Isolates ($0.0001 per action):**  
   Calculations, currency conversions, reminders, and calendar checks execute inside Cloudflare V8 isolates in **2ms**.
3. **Selective Generative Synthesis:**  
   The specialized generative slab (the Frozen Spine) is called **only when the consumer asks for a synthesized deliverable** (e.g., *"Draft an email disputing this invoice"*).

* **Total real infrastructure cost per active consumer:** **~$0.45 / month**.
* **Subscription price:** **$6.99 / month**.
* **Gross Margin:** **93.5%**.

> **We built AVOS as a WhatsApp-native operating system. Consumers text a local phone number, access thousands of specialized, creator-built AI personas (from tax sentries to private tutors), and pay an affordable $5 to $9 a month.**  
> 
> **Because our edge architecture slashes real compute costs to under $0.50 per user, we capture institutional 90%+ software margins on consumer micro-subscriptions, distributed globally with zero customer acquisition cost through our creator marketplace."**

That is an explosive, globally scalable consumer thesis backed by real unit economics.

### The Guiding Principle: "Zero-Superapp Leakage"

When a maritime logistics broker, an M&A lawyer, or an oncology clinic director opens your software, **they must never see a generic "AI chat assistant" or a generic operating system.** 

* To the lawyer, it is **Aegis M&A**: an interactive redlining workbench, deal-closing room, and SEC compliance system.
* To the logistics broker, it is **PortMaster**: a live Bill-of-Lading OCR terminal, automated HS-Code tariff classifier, and EDI customs dispatch system.
* To the oncology clinic, it is **OncoFlow**: a biomarker protocol dashboard, patient WhatsApp triage sentry, and insurance prior-authorization generator.

To make this possible, AVOS cannot just be an API. It must provide an **Extension Developer Kit (`@avos/sdk`)** that exposes every layer of the operating system: **the reactive Canvas runtime, the in-chat MCP-UI widgets, the thread event lifecycle, the storage layer, and the multi-model intelligence fabric.**

---

# Part 1: Three Specialized Vertical Domains (The Native Mental Models)

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                   THE THREE VERTICAL PARADIGMS                                         │
├──────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Domain                   │ The Native Mental Model     │ Core UI Viewports (Canvases & Widgets)        │
├──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1. ⚖️ M&A Legal & Deal   │ "The Deal Closing Room"     │ • Split-pane Word/PDF Redline Diff Engine     │
│    Studio (Aegis M&A)    │ Due diligence, indemnities, │ • Interactive Cap Table & Escrow Formula Grid │
│                          │ disclosure schedules.       │ • Cryptographic Closing Book E-Sign Vault     │
├──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. 🚢 Maritime Customs & │ "The Port Terminal Console" │ • Live Bill of Lading (BoL) Extraction Canvas │
│    Logistics (PortMaster)│ EDI declaration, demurrage, │ • Automated HS-Code Tariff Decision Matrix    │
│                          │ vessel AIS tracking.        │ • WhatsApp Trucker/Captain Dispatch Gateway   │
├──────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. 🩺 Oncology Practice &│ "The Clinical Care Engine"  │ • Biomarker & Chemotherapy Protocol Timeline  │
│    Billing (OncoFlow)    │ Prior auths, ICD-10 coding, │ • Dynamic Insurance Appeal Letter Canvas      │
│                          │ patient symptom triage.     │ • WhatsApp Voice/Symptom Triage Sentry        │
└──────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

### Domain 1: ⚖️ Aegis M&A (Boutique Private Equity & Corporate Law)

#### The User Journey & Native Experience:
1. **Onboarding:** The Managing Partner uploads their firm's standard Delaware M&A Playbook and previous 50 closed deal suites. No complex configuration—Aegis indexes standard fallback clauses into private SQLite vector spaces.
2. **Daily Ingestion:** An incoming 180-page *Stock Purchase Agreement (SPA)* arrives via email. 
3. **The Native Viewport (DocumentRedlineCanvas):** 
   * The screen does not show a blank chatbot. The center and right viewports render an **Interactive Legal Redline Canvas**.
   * High-risk clauses (e.g., uncapped indemnification, non-standard survival periods) are highlighted in amber and red.
   * Clicking a redline opens an inline **Clause Negotiation Card** right inside the document showing the opposing counsel’s language, the firm's approved standard fallback, and an automated risk score.
4. **Execution:** The associate clicks `[ Apply Approved Delaware Compromise ]`. The agent commits the edit directly to the underlying Git Artifact repository (`/workspace/contracts/SPA_v2.docx`), updates the deal closing checklist, and generates a clean signature packet with audit certificates.

---

### Domain 2: 🚢 PortMaster Edge (Maritime Logistics & Customs Clearance)

#### The User Journey & Native Experience:
1. **Onboarding:** A freight forwarder in Rotterdam or Miami connects their WhatsApp Business API and inputs their national customs broker credentials.
2. **The WhatsApp Inbound:** A ship captain or truck driver sends a blurry photo of a handwritten **Bill of Lading (BoL)** and a 10-second voice note: *"Container MSKU-908124 arrived at Pier 4. Consignee needs immediate clearance for refrigerated seafood."*
3. **System 1 JEV Triage (<100ms):** JEV classifies the intent as `CUSTOMS_INBOUND_CLEARANCE`, triggers OCR, and opens a new Task Thread.
4. **The Native Viewport (Tariff & Customs Canvas):**
   * The dispatcher sits at their desktop. The screen displays the **Customs Declaration Workbench**.
   * Left side: Extracted invoice data. Center: **HS-Code Decision Grid** (classifying frozen seafood under HS 0306.17 with exact import tariff percentages). Right: Vessel AIS live radar.
5. **Automated Filing:** The broker clicks `[ Submit EDI Declaration ]`. The system compiles the XML, dispatches it to the national port authority gateway, calculates demurrage risk, and texts the driver a QR-code gate pass on WhatsApp.

---

### Domain 3: 🩺 OncoFlow (Specialized Clinical Oncology Practice)

#### The User Journey & Native Experience:
1. **The WhatsApp Patient Triage:** An oncology patient undergoing chemotherapy texts the clinic’s WhatsApp number at 9:00 PM with an audio note: *"I took my cycle 3 antiemetics, but I have a fever of 101.4°F and extreme nausea."*
2. **Edge Triage & Clinical Gatekeeper:** 
   * System 1 JEV processes the audio in 80ms, flags `NEUTROPENIC_FEVER_CRITICAL_ALERT`, and immediately bypasses standard queues to sound an urgent SMS/WhatsApp alert to the on-call oncologist.
   * It simultaneously creates a structured clinical draft note adhering strictly to **ASCO Chemotherapy Toxicity Guidelines**.
3. **The Native Viewport (Clinical Protocol Canvas):**
   * The doctor opens their mobile web app. They see the **Patient Biomarker Timeline** (WBC, Absolute Neutrophil Count, Liver enzymes graphed over time).
   * The system automatically generates a pre-populated **Urgent Insurance Prior-Authorization Appeal Letter** with exact citations to the patient's pathology reports to approve emergency hospital admission.

---

# Part 2: The AVOS Extension SDK (`@avos/sdk`)

To allow a developer (Forward Deployed Engineer) to build *any* of the three vertical operating systems above, AVOS provides the **`ExtensionContext` (`ctx`)** and its core lifecycle primitives.

```
                                  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                                  │           AVOS EXTENSION RUNTIME             │
                                  └──────────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
                                                         │
         ┌───────────────────────┬───────────────────────┼───────────────────────┬───────────────────────┐
         ▼                       ▼                       ▼                       ▼                       ▼
┌──────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────┐
│   `ctx.agent`    │    │   `ctx.thread`   │    │   `ctx.canvas`   │    │  `ctx.storage`   │    │   `ctx.events`   │
│ • System 1 Jev   │    │ • Interceptors   │    │ • State-Sync     │    │ • DO SQLite      │    │ • Webhooks       │
│ • Frontier Model │    │ • In-Chat Cards  │    │ • CRDT Document  │    │ • R2 Buckets     │    │ • Cron Triggers  │
│ • System Prompts │    │ • Action Buttons │    │ • Custom Panels  │    │ • Git Artifacts  │    │ • WhatsApp Hooks │
└──────────────────┘    └──────────────────┘    └──────────────────┘    └──────────────────┘    └──────────────────┘
```

---

### 1. The Core Extension Context (`ctx`) Specification

Every AVOS extension implements a standard lifecycle: `onLoad()`, `onUnload()`, event listeners, tool registrations, and canvas viewport providers.

```typescript
// packages/sdk/src/types/context.ts

export interface ExtensionContext {
  /** Unique ID of the current tenant space (SpaceDO instance) */
  spaceId: string;
  
  /** Scoped identity of the caller (User, Agent, or External Webhook) */
  caller: CallerIdentity;

  /** 1. THE AGENT & INTELLIGENCE RUNTIME */
  agent: {
    /** Run ultra-fast System 1 non-autoregressive decision (Jev AI) */
    evalSystemOne<T extends string>(params: {
      state: string | Record<string, unknown>;
      question: string;
      choices: Record<T, string>; // Enum choices with semantic criteria
    }): Promise<{ choice: T; confidence: number }>;

    /** Run System 2 Generative Frontier Model with automatic tool-calling */
    generateText(params: {
      model?: "fast" | "reasoning" | "vision";
      systemPrompt: string;
      messages: Message[];
      tools?: ToolDefinition[];
    }): Promise<GenerationResult>;

    /** Register dynamic custom tools exposed to the agent */
    registerTool(tool: ToolDefinition): void;
  };

  /** 2. THE THREAD & IN-CHAT UI SYSTEM */
  thread: {
    /** Intercept messages before they reach the model or user */
    onNewMessage(handler: (msg: IncomingMessage, controls: FlowControl) => Promise<void>): void;

    /** Inject rich interactive MCP-UI widgets directly into the chat stream */
    renderWidget(widget: {
      type: "card" | "action_group" | "diff_preview" | "form";
      title: string;
      data: Record<string, unknown>;
      actions: ActionButton[];
    }): Promise<string>; // Returns widget instance ID

    /** Update an existing in-chat widget dynamically */
    updateWidget(widgetId: string, data: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;
  };

  /** 3. THE INTERACTIVE CANVAS RUNTIME (Right-Side Viewport) */
  canvas: {
    /** Open or focus a specialized domain viewport canvas */
    mount(canvasId: string, config: {
      title: string;
      component: string; // e.g. "aegis.DocumentRedlineCanvas" or "portmaster.TariffGrid"
      initialState: Record<string, unknown>;
    }): Promise<CanvasSession>;

    /** Publish real-time state updates to the active canvas */
    setState(canvasId: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;

    /** Listen for user edits or interactions inside the canvas */
    onAction(canvasId: string, handler: (action: CanvasAction) => Promise<void>): void;
  };

  /** 4. ISOLATED STORAGE & GIT ARTIFACTS */
  storage: {
    /** Scoped SQLite database running on Durable Object local disk */
    sqlite: {
      execute<T = unknown>(sql: string, params?: unknown[]): Promise<T[]>;
      transaction<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
    };

    /** Secure Object Store for large files, PDFs, images (R2 backed) */
    files: {
      read(path: string): Promise<Uint8Array>;
      write(path: string, data: Uint8Array | string, meta?: FileMetadata): Promise<void>;
      getSignedUrl(path: string, expiresInSeconds?: number): Promise<string>;
    };

    /** Git Artifacts Engine (Version-controlled deliverables) */
    git: {
      commit(path: string, content: string, message: string): Promise<string>;
      diff(branchA: string, branchB: string): Promise<FileDiff[]>;
      createBranch(branchName: string): Promise<void>;
    };
  };

  /** 5. EVENT BUS, CRON & OMNICHANNEL TRIGGERS */
  events: {
    /** Register an external webhook endpoint: /api/ext/:extensionId/:path */
    registerWebhook(path: string, handler: (req: Request) => Promise<Response>): void;

    /** Listen for incoming WhatsApp messages / audio notes / photos */
    onWhatsAppMessage(handler: (msg: WhatsAppPayload) => Promise<void>): void;

    /** Schedule background daemons (Cloudflare Workflows) */
    scheduleCron(schedule: string, handler: () => Promise<void>): void;
  };

  /** 6. GATEKEEPER & AUDIT TRAIL */
  security: {
    /** Require human confirmation before executing high-risk actions */
    requestHumanApproval(params: {
      title: string;
      description: string;
      riskScore: number;
    }): Promise<boolean>;

    /** Commit cryptographic audit entry to immutable log */
    auditLog(entry: AuditEntry): Promise<void>;
  };
}
```

---

# Part 3: Concrete Implementation of the 3 Verticals

Here is how an FDE writes the actual vertical extensions using `@avos/sdk`.

---

### Implementation 1: The M&A Legal Extension (`ext_aegis_legal`)

```typescript
// extensions/ext_aegis_legal/src/index.ts
import { ExtensionContext, ExtensionManifest } from "@avos/sdk";

export const manifest: ExtensionManifest = {
  id: "ext_aegis_legal",
  name: "Aegis M&A Deal Studio",
  version: "1.0.0",
  canvases: [
    { id: "redline_viewport", name: "Contract Redlining Engine", entry: "./ui/RedlineCanvas.tsx" },
    { id: "cap_table", name: "Deal Structure & Escrow Grid", entry: "./ui/CapTableGrid.tsx" }
  ]
};

export default function activate(ctx: ExtensionContext) {
  // 1. Initialize custom SQLite table for Deal Metadata
  ctx.storage.sqlite.execute(`
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deal_risks (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      contract_name TEXT,
      clause_type TEXT,
      risk_severity TEXT,
      original_text TEXT,
      suggested_fallback TEXT,
      status TEXT DEFAULT 'PENDING'
    )
  `);

  // 2. Intercept incoming contract files in chat
  ctx.thread.onNewMessage(async (message, flow) => {
    const doc = message.attachments.find(a => a.name.endsWith(".docx") || a.name.endsWith(".pdf"));
    if (!doc) return;

    // Fast-path notification
    await ctx.thread.renderWidget({
      type: "card",
      title: `⚖️ Ingesting M&A Agreement: ${doc.name}`,
      data: { status: "Analyzing indemnities and survival periods..." },
      actions: []
    });

    // 3. Mount the Interactive Redline Canvas
    const canvas = await ctx.canvas.mount("redline_viewport", {
      title: `Redline Audit: ${doc.name}`,
      component: "aegis.DocumentRedlineCanvas",
      initialState: { documentId: doc.id, loading: true }
    });

    // 4. Run System 2 Clause-by-Clause Audit
    const analysis = await ctx.agent.generateText({
      model: "reasoning",
      systemPrompt: "You are an elite M&A lawyer. Audit indemnification and representations according to Delaware standard playbooks.",
      messages: [{ role: "user", content: `Audit this contract: ${doc.url}` }]
    });

    // Push redline state directly to the right-side Canvas viewport
    await ctx.canvas.setState("redline_viewport", {
      loading: false,
      redlines: analysis.data.redlines,
      riskScore: analysis.data.overallRisk
    });
  });

  // 5. Handle user clicking "Accept Delaware Compromise" in the Canvas
  ctx.canvas.onAction("redline_viewport", async (action) => {
    if (action.type === "APPLY_COMPROMISE_CLAUSE") {
      const { clauseId, newText, filePath } = action.payload;

      // Commit change to Git Artifacts
      await ctx.storage.git.commit(filePath, newText, `Aegis: Applied approved fallback for ${clauseId}`);

      // Log to cryptographic audit trail
      await ctx.security.auditLog({
        action: "CONTRACT_CLAUSE_OVERRIDE",
        user: ctx.caller.userId,
        details: { clauseId, newText }
      });
    }
  });
}
```

---

### Implementation 2: The Maritime Logistics Extension (`ext_portmaster_edge`)

```typescript
// extensions/ext_portmaster_edge/src/index.ts
import { ExtensionContext, ExtensionManifest } from "@avos/sdk";

export const manifest: ExtensionManifest = {
  id: "ext_portmaster_edge",
  name: "PortMaster Maritime Logistics",
  version: "1.0.0",
  canvases: [
    { id: "customs_workbench", name: "Customs Clearance & EDI Dispatch", entry: "./ui/CustomsGrid.tsx" }
  ]
};

export default function activate(ctx: ExtensionContext) {
  // 1. Listen for WhatsApp messages from truck drivers & captains
  ctx.events.onWhatsAppMessage(async (msg) => {
    // 2. System 1 Jev AI: Classify intent in 80ms ($0.00004)
    const triage = await ctx.agent.evalSystemOne({
      state: msg.text || msg.caption || "Image/Audio attachment",
      question: "What is the operational logistics category of this message?",
      choices: {
        bol_submission: "Driver sending photo of Bill of Lading document",
        demurrage_inquiry: "Asking about container storage fees and pier deadlines",
        vessel_arrival: "Reporting ship docking status at container terminal",
        general: "Routine communication"
      }
    });

    if (triage.choice === "bol_submission" && msg.mediaUrl) {
      // 3. Mount Customs Declaration Canvas on the Dispatcher's Desktop
      await ctx.canvas.mount("customs_workbench", {
        title: `Bill of Lading: ${msg.senderPhone}`,
        component: "portmaster.TariffGrid",
        initialState: { imageUrl: msg.mediaUrl, processing: true }
      });

      // 4. Extract Cargo & Match HS Tariff Codes
      const extracted = await ctx.agent.generateText({
        model: "vision",
        systemPrompt: "Extract Consignee, Container Number, Cargo Description, and recommend 6-digit HS Codes.",
        messages: [{ role: "user", content: [{ type: "image", url: msg.mediaUrl }] }]
      });

      // Update Canvas with extracted table
      await ctx.canvas.setState("customs_workbench", {
        processing: false,
        containerNumber: extracted.data.containerNumber,
        hsCode: extracted.data.hsCode,
        dutyPayableUSD: extracted.data.dutyAmount
      });
    }
  });

  // 5. Handle EDI Customs Submission from the Canvas
  ctx.canvas.onAction("customs_workbench", async (action) => {
    if (action.type === "SUBMIT_NATIONAL_CUSTOMS_EDI") {
      const { declarationXml } = action.payload;

      // Gatekeeper: Require human approval if duty > $5,000
      const approved = await ctx.security.requestHumanApproval({
        title: "Submit High-Value Customs Declaration",
        description: `Duty payable: $${action.payload.dutyPayableUSD}`,
        riskScore: 0.8
      });

      if (approved) {
        // Dispatch EDI payload to Customs Authority Webhook
        const res = await fetch("https://api.customs.gov/edi/v3/declare", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/xml" },
          body: declarationXml
        });

        // Send confirmation gate pass directly back to driver's WhatsApp
        await ctx.events.onWhatsAppMessage(/* dispatch QR code back */);
      }
    }
  });
}
```

---

### Implementation 3: The Oncology Clinical Practice Extension (`ext_oncoflow`)

```typescript
// extensions/ext_oncoflow/src/index.ts
import { ExtensionContext, ExtensionManifest } from "@avos/sdk";

export const manifest: ExtensionManifest = {
  id: "ext_oncoflow",
  name: "OncoFlow Clinical Care Engine",
  version: "1.0.0",
  canvases: [
    { id: "biomarker_timeline", name: "Patient Biomarker & Chemotherapy Timeline", entry: "./ui/BiomarkerCanvas.tsx" },
    { id: "prior_auth", name: "Insurance Prior Authorization Generator", entry: "./ui/PriorAuthCanvas.tsx" }
  ]
};

export default function activate(ctx: ExtensionContext) {
  // 1. WhatsApp Critical Toxicity Triage Sentry
  ctx.events.onWhatsAppMessage(async (msg) => {
    // Transcribe audio if patient sent a voice note
    const patientTranscript = msg.audioUrl 
      ? await ctx.agent.generateText({ model: "fast", systemPrompt: "Transcribe medical voice note verbatim.", messages: [{ role: "user", content: msg.audioUrl }] })
      : { text: msg.text };

    // System 1 Jev Sentry: Evaluate ASCO Chemotherapy Toxicity Grade in 80ms
    const alertEvaluation = await ctx.agent.evalSystemOne({
      state: patientTranscript.text,
      question: "Is this cancer patient reporting a life-threatening symptom (fever > 100.4F, acute dyspnea, severe chemo toxicity)?",
      choices: {
        emergency_fever: "Patient has fever while on chemotherapy (Neutropenic Sepsis Risk)",
        routine_symptom: "Mild nausea, mild fatigue, general scheduling question",
        prescription_refill: "Requesting medication refill"
      }
    });

    if (alertEvaluation.choice === "emergency_fever") {
      // 2. Immediate Clinical Escalation: Bypass chat queue
      await ctx.thread.renderWidget({
        type: "card",
        title: "🚨 CRITICAL ONCOLOGY ALERT: Neutropenic Fever",
        data: {
          patient: msg.senderName,
          phone: msg.senderPhone,
          symptoms: patientTranscript.text,
          recommendedAction: "Immediate STAT CBC with Differential + Blood Cultures."
        },
        actions: [
          { id: "call_patient", label: "📞 Dial Patient Now", style: "danger" },
          { id: "order_stat_labs", label: "🧪 Order Emergency Lab Panel", style: "primary" }
        ]
      });

      // 3. Mount Prior Authorization Insurance Canvas automatically
      await ctx.canvas.mount("prior_auth", {
        title: `STAT Emergency Admission Auth: ${msg.senderName}`,
        component: "oncoflow.PriorAuthCanvas",
        initialState: {
          icd10: "D70.1 (Chemotherapy-induced neutropenia)",
          cpt: "99215 (High complexity emergency consult)"
        }
      });
    }
  });
}
```
