### 4. Step 2: Tier 2.5 Programmatic Landing Page Generation

When the agent discovers a cluster of high-intent search terms (e.g. queries around *"SOC 2 compliance for healthtech startups"*), it doesn't send them to the root homepage.

It uses Naelao’s **Tier 2.5 Dynamic Edge SSR** to generate and publish an exact-match landing page in milliseconds:

```typescript
// extensions/ext_growth_core/src/LandingPageCompiler.ts
import { NaelaoContext } from "@naelao/sdk";

export async function generateTargetedLandingPage(
  ctx: NaelaoContext,
  cluster: { keyword: string; industry: string; targetAudience: string }
) {
  // System 2 Generative Model drafts the tailored headlines and social proof
  const copy = await ctx.agent.generateText({
    model: "fast",
    systemPrompt: "You are a conversion rate optimization expert. Write high-converting hero copy matching this exact search intent.",
    messages: [{ role: "user", content: `Cluster: ${JSON.stringify(cluster)}` }]
  });

  // Compiles and publishes directly to Cloudflare Pages edge at a dedicated path
  const pageSlug = `/l/${cluster.keyword.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")}`;
  
  await ctx.ui.createDynamicPage({
    slug: pageSlug,
    template: "conversion-landing-page",
    props: {
      headline: copy.data.headline,
      subheadline: copy.data.subheadline,
      callToAction: "Start 14-Day Free Audit",
      targetedIndustry: cluster.industry,
      badgeText: `Tailored for ${cluster.targetAudience}`
    }
  });

  return { publishedUrl: `https://app.acme.avos.run${pageSlug}` };
}
```

### 5. Step 3: Self-Modifying Code (The Nightly Commit)

The unique aspect of Schneider’s approach was that **the agent rewrites its own bidding and campaign software code** [1, 2].

In Naelao, marketing rules aren't trapped in proprietary SaaS dashboards. They live as **TypeScript code files in the project’s Git Artifacts repository**:
* `/workspace/marketing/bidding_rules.ts`
* `/workspace/marketing/campaign_topology.json`

Every night, after calculating which ad sets drove real Stripe revenue (not just vanity clicks), the agent edits `/workspace/marketing/bidding_rules.ts`:

```typescript
// Automatically modified by Agent via Edit() tool:
export const CAMPAIGN_RULES = {
  maxCpcCeilingUSD: 14.50, // Dropped from $22.00 because conversion rate softened
  targetRoasFloor: 3.2,
  activeLandingPages: [
    "/l/hipaa-dental-texas",
    "/l/soc2-fintech-fast"
  ],
  biddingStrategy: "TARGET_CPA"
};
```

1. The agent writes the patch via `Edit()`.
2. The Zig-WASM Git engine commits the diff with full cognitive metadata:  
   `Commit: c_8812f ("Auto-rebalance: Lowered CPC ceiling for dental cluster, added 42 negatives")`.
3. The next day, the desktop shell renders an interactive **Marketing Performance Card** in the Team Room:
   ```
   ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
   │ 🎯 Autonomous Growth Sentry: Nightly Cycle Complete                    │
   │ • Budget Saved: $410 (Added 28 Negative Keywords)                      │
   │ • New Programmatic Landing Pages: 2 Deployed (/l/hipaa-dental-texas)   │
   │ • 30-Day CPL Trend: $840 ──► $310 [View Live Ads Canvas]               │
   └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
   ```
